# macOS

Every project across all 39 lists that runs on macOS — Apple silicon or Intel — **7,363** of 8,445 distinct repos, deduplicated to one row per repo.

Confirmed first, then inferred, each block by stars. Confirmation means a .dmg/.pkg asset or explicit README prose; inference from a portable runtime alone only ever earns Likely.

[← back to the mega list](../README.md) · [5,877 inferred →](macos-inferred.md)

Page **2** of 3, because this list is longer than the 512 KB GitHub will render in one file. In order: [1](macos.md) · **2** · [3](macos-3.md) — [continue on page 3 →](macos-3.md)
